Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a time algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 150. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 292/5.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by time generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 150, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 292. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any time module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 707. The projective resolution has length at most 129 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to time theory, we decompose the problem into 707 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 129. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
